==========================================================================================

HRS 2009 Internet

Note to Users: This codebook is designed to match the distribution dataset. 
Codebook metadata are derived from the data collection instrument.
If you have questions concerning codebook layout or content please bring
them to the attention of the HRS staff at hrsquest@isr.umich.edu.

Printing recommendation: Set margins (left/right/top/bottom) to .5 inch; print
in portrait orientation using a mono-space 10-point font.

==========================================================================================

C059_09                       POSITION ON SOCIAL LADDER
         Section: I     Level: RESPONDENT      Type: Numeric    Width: 2   Decimals: 0
         Ref: H_C059_

         top of the ladder are the people who are the best off -- those who have the most
         money, most education, and best jobs. At the bottom are the people who are the
         worst off -- who have the least money, least education, and the worst jobs or no
         jobs. The higher up you are on this ladder, the closer you are to the people at
         the very top and the lower you are, the closer you are to the people at the very
         bottom.
         
         Please click on the rung on the ladder where you would place yourself in
         society, with those at the top of the ladder being the best off and those at the
         bottom being the worst off.

C060_09                       POSITION ON SOCIAL LADDER CHANGED
         Section: I     Level: RESPONDENT      Type: Numeric    Width: 1   Decimals: 0
         Ref: C060_

         Think of this ladder as representing where people stand in our society. At the
         top of the ladder are the people who are the best off -- those who have the most
         money, most education, and best jobs. At the bottom are the people who are the
         worst off -- who have the least money, least education, and the worst jobs or no
         jobs. The higher up you are on this ladder, the closer you are to the people at
